# Design inventory

Every item of the design export that the build must match (Phase 8, part B). Generated by `pnpm design:inventory` from `design/inventory.manual.ts` (hand-checked constants, timelines, bindings) and `design/extracted/*.json` (`pnpm design:extract`: copy, assets, controls, bindings, logic lines). Statuses live in `design/inventory.status.json`. Source references: `<page>/template.html:<line>` = `design/unpacked/<page>/template.html` (`pnpm design:unpack`).

Notes on files (B1): the exports were already in `design/export/` (moved in Phase 7, commit c36aa63, names kept with spaces), and no `Saakshi_Design_System.html` (v2) exists, so nothing was archived.

**1859 items.** Status: todo 1856, built 0, verified 0, verified-with-note 3.

## Design vs product truth (B5): the product wins

| Binding | Rule | Resolution | Inventory ids |
|---|---|---|---|
| B5.1 | Trust rules | SK.score weights (10/25/25/25/15, bands 80/40, screen photo = hard fail) are placeholders. Every TrustMeter, ledger, band, simulator and preset uses lib/trust (bands 75/45, ticks at 45 and 75, our reason sentences, screen photo = review flag). | D-0016, D-0025, D-0040, D-0044, D-0052, D-0060, D-0145, D-0163, D-0170, D-0191, D-0202, D-0204, D-0215, D-0216, D-0224, D-0231, D-0232, D-0240, D-0247, D-0314, … |
| B5.2 | Fingerprint | Glyphs render from our pHash hex; 'N of 64 cells differ' is the real hamming distance; capture previews a browser port of lib/phash.ts, then the server value. | D-0043, D-1193, D-1343 |
| B5.3 | Data | SAAKSHI_ARCHIVE never enters the product DB or public routes (only /dev/parity). Real routes bind to our DB; credits from our DB. | D-0085, D-0311, D-0312, D-0313, D-0804, D-0805, D-0806, D-0807, D-0808, D-0925, D-1036, D-1239, D-1511, D-1512 |
| B5.4 | Numbers and names | Every number/name in design copy binds to data (hero facts, project names/counts/years, report claims, reason templates with the cached reverse geocoder). Missing data → designed empty state, never a sample. | D-0043, D-0051, D-0059, D-0072, D-0078, D-0342, D-0343, D-0344, D-0345, D-0346, D-0347, D-0348, D-0349, D-0350, D-0351, D-0352, D-0353, D-0354, D-0355, D-0356, … |
| B5.5 | Chapter 10 parameters | From docs/external-apis.md (what we really call), not image_metadata/detection/auto_tagging. | D-0084, D-0643 |
| B5.6 | Chapter 6 | /api/demo/tamper extended to remove any chip (signature, crop, blur_faces, format, public id) and return the real status for each. | D-0073 |
| B5.7 | Chapter 8 | /api/demo/try (real pipeline, sandbox at the stage venue), not a browser hash. | D-0079 |
| B5.8 | Live arrivals | Chapter 9 and the Wall use /api/live. Simulation only in operator mode (?operator=1 + DEMO_ADMIN_SECRET), labelled 'Rehearsal: simulated arrival'; autoSimulate off in production. | D-0080, D-0515, D-0516, D-0517, D-0518, D-0519, D-0520, D-0530, D-0531, D-0633, D-0634, D-0635, D-0636, D-1080, D-1081 |
| B5.9 | History intact | GET /api/audit/chain?assetId=; the browser recomputes with Web Crypto SHA-256 in lib/hashchain.ts's canonical format; the server chain stays authoritative. | D-0853 |
| B5.10 | Map dot field | Regenerated from Natural Earth 50 m land at 0.2° for lat 6–30 °N, lng 68–92 °E; land dots only, never boundaries; any MapLibre view strips boundary layers and stays at zoom ≥ 10. | D-0052, D-0523, D-1334 |
| B5.11 | Review annotations | 'Chapter N, P0' marks, sample labels and showMarks/showSample/showP1 never ship; motion → auto-detection (+ dev ?motion=); demoState → dev ?state=. | D-0025, D-0145, D-0163, D-0170, D-0191, D-0202, D-0204, D-0215, D-0216, D-0224, D-0231, D-0232, D-0240, D-0247, D-0512, D-0513, D-0514, D-0515, D-0516, D-0517, … |

| Component | States | Used in | File |

| AppShell, Rail | expanded, collapsed, mobile sheet; Review count badge | App | `components/app-shell.tsx` |
| DemoBanner | default, resetting | App | `components/demo-banner.tsx` |
| CommandPalette | closed, open, empty results, keyboard highlight | App, ⌘K | `components/command-palette.tsx` |
| TrustMeter | checking, counting, Verified, Needs review, Flagged; hard-fail panel | Landing, How it works, Capture, Wall | `components/trust-meter.tsx` |
| ProofStrip | unsealed, sealing (press + fill), sealed | Landing, Evidence, Review | `components/proof-strip.tsx` |
| Ledger | full, partial, zero, bad; total row | Evidence, Review, Drawer | `components/ledger.tsx` |
| Glyph | plain, night, diff (cells lit), building cell by cell | Everywhere | `components/glyph.tsx` |
| BandPin, Cluster | Verified circle, Needs review diamond, Flagged triangle; cluster with band bar; focus | Library map, Wall | `components/band-pin.tsx` |
| PhotoTile | default, hover (glyph), selected, flagged outline, rejected (desaturated), loading skeleton | Library, Review, Projects | `components/photo-tile.tsx` |
| SearchChips | empty, typing, chips (band, project, year, no location, text), backspace removes last | Library | `components/search-chips.tsx` |
| BulkBar | hidden, n selected, action pending | Library | `components/bulk-bar.tsx` |
| EvidenceViewer | collapsed, exploded, single layer, loupe (P1) | Drawer, Evidence page | `components/evidence-viewer.tsx` |
| EvidenceDrawer | Trust, Facts, History, Duplicates, Credits; loading; error | App | `components/evidence-drawer.tsx` |
| ReviewItem | current, queued, note missing (error), approving, rejecting, decided | Review | `components/review-item.tsx` |
| Thread | hidden, drawing, lit, dimmed | Landing, Report, Projects, Studio | `components/thread.tsx` |
| KpiCard | default, hover (threads), sample value, no data | Projects, Report | `components/kpi-card.tsx` |
| MaskSweep | idle, sweeping, shown; threshold (How it works) | Landing, Projects, How it works | `components/mask-sweep.tsx` |
| TimeScrubber | before, after, check-in n, latest | Spot page, landing ch. 7 | `components/time-scrubber.tsx` |
| CaptureHUD | acquiring, good fix, low accuracy, no location; level, tilted; inside, near edge | Capture | `components/capture-hud.tsx` |
| PipelineSheet | Uploading, Reading, Checking, Scored; Queued offline; done | Capture, Wall card | `components/pipeline-sheet.tsx` |
| TemplateCanvas | stat, split, photo; exporting; exported URL | Studio | `components/template-canvas.tsx` |
| StateBlock | empty, loading, error, offline banner | All app screens | `components/state-block.tsx` |
